    66                                                 task automatic check_irq_status(input bit exp_irq,
    67                                                                                 input bit exp_rx_empty,
    68                                                                                 input int unsigned idle_cycles,
    69                                                                                 input string step_name);
    70                                                   bit [31:0] status;
    71                                               
    72              1                         24         apb_read(ADDR_STATUS, status, idle_cycles);
    73                                                   if (status[4] != exp_irq || status[2] != exp_rx_empty) begin
    74                                                     `uvm_error("IRQ_STATUS",
    75                                                                $sformatf("%s: STATUS=0x%08h, expected irq=%0b rx_empty=%0b",
    76              1                    ***0***                                step_name, status, exp_irq, exp_rx_empty))
    77                                                   end
    78                                                 endtask
    79                                               
    80                                                 task body();
    81                                                   bit [31:0] data;
    82                                               
    83              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_BAUD, 32'd1, 1);
    84                                               
    85                                                   // Keep loopback enabled while IRQ is disabled. The received byte must stay
    86                                                   // in the FIFO without asserting the interrupt.
    87              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h3, 4);
    88              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 4, "reset state with IRQ disabled");
    89              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_TXDATA, 32'ha5, 2);
    90              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b0, 360, "RX pending with IRQ disabled");
    91                                               
    92                                                   // Enabling IRQ while data is already pending must assert it immediately.
    93              1                          3         apb_write(ADDR_CTRL, 32'h7, 2);
    94              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b1, 1'b0, 2, "enable IRQ with pending data");
    95              1                          3         apb_read(ADDR_RXDATA, data, 2);
    96                                                   if (data[7:0] != 8'ha5) begin
    97              1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("IRQ_DATA", $sformatf("read 0x%02h expected 0xa5", data[7:0]))
    98                                                   end
    99              1                          3         check_irq_status(1'b0, 1'b1, 8, "FIFO empty after first read");
    100                                              
    101                                                  // With IRQ enabled, a new RX byte must assert it. Disabling IRQ must lower

    142                                              
    143                                                task automatic wait_for_config(input bit [2:0] expected_ctrl,
    144                                                                               input bit [31:0] expected_baud,
    145                                                                               input string check_name);
    146                                                  bit matched;
    147                                                  fork
    148                                                    begin
    149                                                      wait (probe_vif.uart_clk_rst_n && !probe_vif.cfg_busy &&
    150                                                            (probe_vif.ctrl_uart_cfg == expected_ctrl) &&
    151             1                         12                   (probe_vif.baud_uart_cfg == expected_baud));
    152             1                         12             matched = 1'b1;
    153                                                    end
    154                                                    begin
    155             1                          9             #4us;
    156                                                    end
    157                                                  join_any
    158             1                         12         disable fork;
    159                                                  if (!matched) begin
    160                                                    `uvm_error("CFG_STRESS",
    161                                                               $sformatf("%s did not converge: busy=%0b pending=%0b ctrl=0x%0h baud=%0d",
    162                                                                         check_name, probe_vif.cfg_busy, probe_vif.cfg_pending,
    163             1                    ***0***                                probe_vif.ctrl_uart_cfg, probe_vif.baud_uart_cfg))
    164                                                  end
    165                                                endtask
    166                                              
    167                                                task run_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    168                                                  uart_config_burst_seq burst;
    169                                                  uart_config_write_seq write_seq;
    170                                                  bit saw_busy_write;
    171                                              
    172             1                          3         phase.raise_objection(this);
    173                                                  wait (probe_vif.pclk_rst_n && probe_vif.uart_clk_rst_n &&
    174             1                          3               probe_vif.cfg_uart_initialized);
    175                                              
    176                                                  fork
    177                                                    begin
    178             1                          3             forever begin
    179             1                         48               @(posedge probe_vif.pclk);
    180                                                        if (probe_vif.cfg_write && probe_vif.cfg_busy) begin
    181             1                          9                 saw_busy_write = 1'b1;
    182                                                        end
    183                                                      end
    184                                                    end
    185                                                    begin
    186             1                          3             burst = uart_config_burst_seq::type_id::create("burst");
    187             1                          3             burst.start(env.apb.seqr);
    188                                                    end
    189                                                  join_any
    190             1                          3         disable fork;
    191                                              
    192             1                          3         wait_for_config(3'b011, 32'd9, "back-to-back writes");
    193                                                  if (!saw_busy_write) begin
    194             1                    ***0***           `uvm_error("CFG_STRESS", "the burst did not exercise a write while the mailbox was busy")
    195                                                  end
    196                                              
    197                                                  // Reset the APB side while a request is in flight. Both visible registers
    198                                                  // and the effective UART-domain copy must return to their specified reset
    199                                                  // values; stale pending data must not be applied afterwards.
    200             1                          3         write_seq = uart_config_write_seq::type_id::create("pre_apb_reset_write");
    201             1                          3         write_seq.cfg_addr = UART_ADDR_BAUD;
    202             1                          3         write_seq.cfg_data = 32'd13;
    203             1                          3         write_seq.start(env.apb.seqr);
    204             1                          3         wait (probe_vif.cfg_busy || probe_vif.cfg_apply_uart);
    205             1                          3         reset_vif.pulse_apb_reset(3);
    206             1                          3         wait_for_config(UART_CTRL_RESET[2:0], UART_BAUD_RESET, "APB reset recovery");
    207                                              
    208                                                  // UART-only reset keeps the APB register file alive. The mailbox startup
    209                                                  // handshake must restore the retained APB configuration exactly once.
    210             1                          3         burst = uart_config_burst_seq::type_id::create("retained_burst");
    211             1                          3         burst.final_ctrl = 3'b110;
    212             1                          3         burst.final_baud = 32'd11;
    213             1                          3         burst.start(env.apb.seqr);
    214             1                          3         wait_for_config(3'b110, 32'd11, "pre UART-only reset");
    215             1                          3         reset_vif.pulse_uart_reset(3);
    216             1                          3         wait_for_config(3'b110, 32'd11, "UART-only reset recovery");
    217                                              
    218                                                  `uvm_info("CFG_STRESS",
    219                                                            "busy coalescing and APB/UART reset recovery converged to the final register values",
    220             1                          3                   UVM_LOW)
    221             1                          3         #500ns;
    222             1                          3         phase.drop_objection(this);
    223                                                endtask
    224                                              endclass

    1                                                class uart_coverage extends uvm_component;
    2               1                    ***0***       `uvm_component_utils(uart_coverage)
    2               2                    ***0***     
    2               3                    ***0***     
    3                                                
    4                                                  uvm_analysis_imp_apb_cov  #(apb_item,  uart_coverage) apb_export;
    5                                                  uvm_analysis_imp_uart_cov #(uart_item, uart_coverage) uart_export;
    6                                                  uvm_analysis_imp_reset_cov #(uart_reset_item, uart_coverage) reset_export;
    7                                                
    8                                                  bit [7:0]  cov_addr;
    9                                                  bit        cov_write;
    10                                                 bit        cov_slverr;
    11                                                 bit [7:0]  cov_data;
    12                                                 bit        cov_irq_en;
    13                                                 bit        cov_status_rx_full;
    14                                                 bit        cov_status_rx_empty;
    15                                                 bit        cov_status_irq;
    16                                                 bit        cov_status_frame_err;
    17                                                 bit [1:0]  cov_reset_kind;
    18                                               
    19                                                 covergroup apb_cg;
    20                                                   option.per_instance = 1;
    21                                               
    22                                                   cp_addr: coverpoint cov_addr {
    23                                                     bins ctrl   = {UART_ADDR_CTRL};
    24                                                     bins status = {UART_ADDR_STATUS};
    25                                                     bins baud   = {UART_ADDR_BAUD};
    26                                                     bins txdata = {UART_ADDR_TXDATA};
    27                                                     bins rxdata = {UART_ADDR_RXDATA};
    28                                                     bins bad    = default;
    29                                                   }
    30                                               
    31                                                   cp_kind: coverpoint cov_write {
    32                                                     bins read  = {0};
    33                                                     bins write = {1};
    34                                                   }
    35                                               
    36                                                   cp_err: coverpoint cov_slverr {
    37                                                     bins ok  = {0};
    38                                                     bins err = {1};
    39                                                   }
    40                                               
    41                                                   cp_data: coverpoint cov_data {
    42                                                     bins zero = {8'h00};
    43                                                     bins ff   = {8'hff};
    44                                                     bins low  = {[8'h01:8'h3f]};
    45                                                     bins mid  = {[8'h40:8'hbf]};
    46                                                     bins high = {[8'hc0:8'hfe]};
    47                                                   }
    48                                               
    49                                                   cross cp_addr, cp_kind;
    50                                                   cross_addr_err: cross cp_addr, cp_err {
    51                                                     ignore_bins legal_register_errors =
